data-canonical-src=\"https://img.shields.io/badge/Hindi-d9d9d9\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a>\n</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Dify is an open-source LLM app development platform. Its intuitive interface combines AI workflow, RAG pipeline, agent capabilities, model management, observability features (including <a href=\"https://www.comet.com/docs/opik/integrations/dify\" rel=\"nofollow\">Opik</a>, <a href=\"https://docs.langfuse.com\" rel=\"nofollow\">Langfuse</a>, and <a href=\"https://docs.arize.com/phoenix\" rel=\"nofollow\">Arize Phoenix</a>) and more, letting you quickly go from prototype to production. Here's a list of the core features:</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Quick start</h2><a id=\"user-content-quick-start\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Quick start\" href=\"#quick-start\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Before installing Dify, make sure your machine meets the following minimum system requirements:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>CPU &gt;= 2 Core</li>\n<li>RAM &gt;= 4 GiB</li>\n</ul>\n</blockquote>\n<br>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The easiest way to start the Dify server is through <a href=\"/langgenius/dify/blob/main/docker/docker-compose.yaml\">Docker Compose</a>. Before running Dify with the following commands, make sure that <a href=\"https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Docker</a> and <a href=\"https://docs.docker.com/compose/install/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Docker Compose</a> are installed on your machine:</p>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\"><pre><span class=\"pl-c1\">cd</span> dify\n<span class=\"pl-c1\">cd</span> docker\ncp .env.example .env\ndocker compose up -d</pre><div class=\"zeroclipboard-container\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy Reach out to <a href=\"#community--contact\">the community and us</a> if you are still having issues.</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">If you'd like to contribute to Dify or do additional development, refer to our <a href=\"https://docs.dify.ai/getting-started/install-self-hosted/local-source-code\" rel=\"nofollow\">guide to deploying from source code</a></p>\n</blockquote>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Key features</h2><a id=\"user-content-key-features\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Key features\" href=\"#key-features\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>1. Workflow</strong>:\nBuild and test powerful AI workflows on a visual canvas, leveraging all the following features and beyond.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>2. Comprehensive model support</strong>:\nSeamless integration with hundreds of proprietary / open-source LLMs from dozens of inference providers and self-hosted solutions, covering GPT, Mistral, Llama3, and any OpenAI API-compatible models. A full list of supported model providers can be found <a href=\"https://docs.dify.ai/getting-started/readme/model-providers\" rel=\"nofollow\">here</a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://private-user-images.githubusercontent.com/13230914/321881730-5a17bdbe-097a-4100-8363-40255b70f6e3.png?jwt=eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJnaXRodWIuY29tIiwiYXVkIjoicmF3LmdpdGh1YnVzZXJjb250ZW50LmNvbSIsImtleSI6ImtleTUiLCJleHAiOjE3ODEzNzUzNzIsIm5iZiI6MTc4MTM3NTA3MiwicGF0aCI6Ii8xMzIzMDkxNC8zMjE4ODE3MzAtNWExN2JkYmUtMDk3YS00MTAwLTgzNjMtNDAyNTViNzBmNmUzLnBuZz9YLUFtei1BbGdvcml0aG09QVdTNC1ITUFDLVNIQTI1NiZYLUFtei1DcmVkZW50aWFsPUFLSUFWQ09EWUxTQTUzUFFLNFpBJTJGMjAyNjA2MTMlMkZ1cy1lYXN0LTElMkZzMyUyRmF3czRfcmVxdWVzdCZYLUFtei1EYXRlPTIwMjYwNjEzVDE4MjQzMlomWC1BbXotRXhwaXJlcz0zMDAmWC1BbXotU2lnbmF0dXJlPTQwOGU2MzYzZjRlMTk0NzQ5NmQyZGRmYTYzMjRiOTJhZWU0MjA3YTVlZGZkNGQ2MzM0NmViNDdhMjE0YWZiY2UmWC1BbXotU2lnbmVkSGVhZGVycz1ob3N0JnJlc3BvbnNlLWNvbnRlbnQtdHlwZT1pbWFnZSUyRnBuZyJ9.m3Nx2mB6c1jnxbekvAHTl6zEz82NWIq9srW5SaCA42g\"><img src=\"https://private-user-images.githubusercontent.com/13230914/321881730-5a17bdbe-097a-4100-8363-40255b70f6e3.png?jwt=eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJnaXRodWIuY29tIiwiYXVkIjoicmF3LmdpdGh1YnVzZXJjb250ZW50LmNvbSIsImtleSI6ImtleTUiLCJleHAiOjE3ODEzNzUzNzIsIm5iZiI6MTc4MTM3NTA3MiwicGF0aCI6Ii8xMzIzMDkxNC8zMjE4ODE3MzAtNWExN2JkYmUtMDk3YS00MTAwLTgzNjMtNDAyNTViNzBmNmUzLnBuZz9YLUFtei1BbGdvcml0aG09QVdTNC1ITUFDLVNIQTI1NiZYLUFtei1DcmVkZW50aWFsPUFLSUFWQ09EWUxTQTUzUFFLNFpBJTJGMjAyNjA2MTMlMkZ1cy1lYXN0LTElMkZzMyUyRmF3czRfcmVxdWVzdCZYLUFtei1EYXRlPTIwMjYwNjEzVDE4MjQzMlomWC1BbXotRXhwaXJlcz0zMDAmWC1BbXotU2lnbmF0dXJlPTQwOGU2MzYzZjRlMTk0NzQ5NmQyZGRmYTYzMjRiOTJhZWU0MjA3YTVlZGZkNGQ2MzM0NmViNDdhMjE0YWZiY2UmWC1BbXotU2lnbmVkSGVhZGVycz1ob3N0JnJlc3BvbnNlLWNvbnRlbnQtdHlwZT1pbWFnZSUyRnBuZyJ9.m3Nx2mB6c1jnxbekvAHTl6zEz82NWIq9srW5SaCA42g\" alt=\"providers-v5\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>3. Prompt IDE</strong>:\nIntuitive interface for crafting prompts, comparing model performance, and adding additional features such as text-to-speech to a chat-based app.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>4. RAG Pipeline</strong>:\nExtensive RAG capabilities that cover everything from document ingestion to retrieval, with out-of-box support for text extraction from PDFs, PPTs, and other common document formats.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>5. Agent capabilities</strong>:\nYou can define agents based on LLM Function Calling or ReAct, and add pre-built or custom tools for the agent. Dify provides 50+ built-in tools for AI agents, such as Google Search, DALL·E, Stable Diffusion and WolframAlpha.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>6. LLMOps</strong>:\nMonitor and analyze application logs and performance over time. You could continuously improve prompts, datasets, and models based on production data and annotations.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>7. Backend-as-a-Service</strong>:\nAll of Dify's offerings come with corresponding APIs, so you could effortlessly integrate Dify into your own business logic.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Using Dify</h2><a id=\"user-content-using-dify\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Using Dify\" href=\"#using-dify\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Cloud <br></strong>\nWe host a <a href=\"https://dify.ai\" rel=\"nofollow\">Dify Cloud</a> service for anyone to try with zero setup.
